Tell us what you would infer

48 cards · 8 reviewers

What we are trying to build

The system exists to do two things on every well: say what is wrong with it, and propose work worth doing with a number attached. So when you answer below, aim at those two ends. An observation that cannot eventually support a diagnosis or a job is still worth writing down — but tell us which of the two you think it serves.

One worked example, in the same shape as your cards

Below is a card filled in as an example, on the same producer whose rows you will see on every card. It has everything your cards have — the dataset, a sentence about it, the rows, the SQL — and the two answer boxes filled. The rows and the numbers are real; the wording is ours, written to show the shape and length we are after. It has everything your cards have — the dataset, a sentence about it, the rows, the SQL — and the two answer boxes filled. It is here to show the shape and length of a useful answer, not to suggest what yours should say.

The SQL that produced thisevery card has this too
SELECT
    cosf.opnl_stat_on_indc,
    cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm,
    cosf.opnl_stat_term_dttm,
    ROUND(NVL(cosf.opnl_stat_term_dttm, SYSDATE) - cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm, 1) AS status_days,
    cosf.off_rsn_type_cde,
    cosf.off_rsn_type_desc,
    cosf.off_rsn_sub_type_cde,
    orst.off_rsn_sub_type_desc,
    cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm,
    cosf.off_rsn_term_dttm,
    ROUND(NVL(cosf.off_rsn_term_dttm, SYSDATE) - cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm, 1) AS off_rsn_days,
    ortd.acty_grp AS activity_group,
    ortd.opnl_cndn_off_clsn_cde AS off_classification
FROM dwrptg.cmpl_opnl_stat_fact cosf
LEFT JOIN dwrptg.off_rsn_sub_type_dmn orst
    ON cosf.off_rsn_sub_type_cde = orst.off_rsn_sub_type_cde
LEFT JOIN dwrptg.off_rsn_type_dmn ortd
    ON cosf.off_rsn_type_cde = ortd.off_rsn_type_cde
WHERE cosf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
  AND cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -24)
ORDER BY cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm DESC, cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm DESC

2 · Your readon your own cards these two boxes are empty

How should the model read this?What are you looking at first, what pattern matters, and what would you ignore as noise? Write it the way you would explain it to a new engineer.
Four things come out of these columns, and none of them from reading a single row.

How often the well goes down. Count the off-spells — the rows where OPNL_STAT_ON_INDC is N — over the window, and total their OFF_RSN_DAYS. Frequency and total time lost are separate measures and both are worth having: a well that trips briefly every month is a different problem from one that went down once and stayed down.

What it goes down for. Group the off-spells by OFF_RSN_TYPE_CDE and OFF_RSN_SUB_TYPE_CDE. Report the code that appears most often, and separately the code that accounts for the most days — they are frequently not the same code, and naming only one of them points at the wrong problem.

When it was last off, and when it last came back on. The most recent rows give the latest transition in each direction and how long it lasted.

What the current status is, and how long it has stood. The row with no OPNL_STAT_TERM_DTTM is the live one; STATUS_DAYS says how long it has held. Everything carrying a termination date is history. A very young current status means “running” is true but not yet meaningful.
What should it take away?The one or two things worth carrying forward from this dataset alone — the sentence you would want written down about this well.
Two things: downtime frequency and the dominant down reason. How often this well goes off and how much time that costs, which reason code accounts for most of it, and whether it is on or off right now.
Why it reads like this. Notice it describes what to work out, not what this particular well shows. It names the columns it would use, it separates two measures that are easy to conflate (how often versus how long), and it says which row counts as current. Written that way it applies to any well — which is what we can actually build from.

So please do not analyse the sample rows. They are there to show you the shape of the data and to argue with if a column looks wrong. What we want in the boxes is the rule you would apply to every well, at roughly this length.

The three questions

  1. How should the model read this? What are you looking at first, what pattern matters, and what would you ignore as noise? Write it the way you would explain it to a new engineer.
  2. What should it take away? The one or two things worth carrying forward from this dataset alone — the sentence you would want written down about this well.
  3. How do these datasets come together? You have read them one at a time. Now: what combination of them tells you what is actually wrong with a well, and what combination would make you propose a specific job — with a rough idea of what it is worth? Just as useful: what combination would stop you recommending work. Every other group's datasets are in the left sidebar and you are welcome to reach into them — the best answers here usually cross groups. Asked once per group, at the end.

Sample data comes from these two wells

One producer and one injector, so both sides of any rule you describe have real rows behind them. These are actual query results from a real run, not illustrations. Where a dataset is empty for one well that is genuine — either it does not apply to that well type, or the well has none of that record.

SELECT
    cosf.opnl_stat_on_indc,
    cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm,
    cosf.opnl_stat_term_dttm,
    ROUND(NVL(cosf.opnl_stat_term_dttm, SYSDATE) - cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm, 1) AS status_days,
    cosf.off_rsn_type_cde,
    cosf.off_rsn_type_desc,
    cosf.off_rsn_sub_type_cde,
    orst.off_rsn_sub_type_desc,
    cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm,
    cosf.off_rsn_term_dttm,
    ROUND(NVL(cosf.off_rsn_term_dttm, SYSDATE) - cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm, 1) AS off_rsn_days,
    ortd.acty_grp AS activity_group,
    ortd.opnl_cndn_off_clsn_cde AS off_classification
FROM dwrptg.cmpl_opnl_stat_fact cosf
LEFT JOIN dwrptg.off_rsn_sub_type_dmn orst
    ON cosf.off_rsn_sub_type_cde = orst.off_rsn_sub_type_cde
LEFT JOIN dwrptg.off_rsn_type_dmn ortd
    ON cosf.off_rsn_type_cde = ortd.off_rsn_type_cde
WHERE cosf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
  AND cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -24)
ORDER BY cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm DESC, cosf.off_rsn_eftv_dttm DESC

SELECT
    cmf.eftv_dttm,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS oil_bopd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS gross_bfpd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS water_bwpd,
    CASE WHEN cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
         THEN ROUND((cmf.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty / cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty) * 100, 1)
         ELSE NULL END AS water_cut_pct,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS steam_total_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_cnts_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS steam_cnts_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS steam_cycl_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS water_inj_bwpd,
    ROUND(cmf.tgt_engr_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS eng_target_steam_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.tgt_opns_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS ops_target_steam_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.tgt_engr_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS eng_target_water_bwpd,
    ROUND(cmf.tgt_opns_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS ops_target_water_bwpd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_stm_inj_on_days_rte_qty, 1) AS steam_on_days_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_cnts_stm_inj_on_days_rte, 1) AS steam_cnts_on_days_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_on_days_rte, 1) AS steam_cycl_on_days_bspd,
    ROUND(cmf.aloc_wtr_inj_on_days_rte_qty, 1) AS water_inj_on_days_bwpd,
    ROUND(cmf.avg_wlhd_tbg_prsr_qty, 1) AS avg_tbg_press_psig,
    ROUND(cmf.avg_wlhd_csg_prsr_qty, 1) AS avg_csg_press_psig,
    ROUND(cmf.avg_wlhd_tbg_temp_qty, 1) AS avg_tbg_temp_f,
    ROUND(cmf.avg_wlhd_csg_temp_qty, 1) AS avg_csg_temp_f
FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact cmf
WHERE cmf.cmpl_dmn_key = :cmpl_dmn_key
  AND cmf.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -24)
ORDER BY cmf.eftv_dttm DESC

Life-of-well monthly production/injection aggregated across all completions (up to 15 years) — oil, gross, water, steam types, water inj, flowline temp, uptime

Injector · OR5580-12

180 row(s), first 3 shown

EFTV_DTTMOIL_BOPDGROSS_BFPDWATER_BWPDWC_PCTSTEAM_BSPDSTEAM_CNTS_BSPDSTEAM_CYCL_BSPDWATER_INJ_BWPDFLT_FUPTIME_DAYS

SELECT
    f.eftv_dttm,
    SUM(f.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty) AS oil_bopd,
    SUM(f.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty) AS gross_bfpd,
    SUM(f.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty) AS water_bwpd,
    ROUND(SUM(f.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty) / NULLIF(SUM(f.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty), 0) * 100, 1) AS wc_pct,
    SUM(f.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty) AS steam_bspd,
    SUM(f.aloc_cnts_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty) AS steam_cnts_bspd,
    SUM(f.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty) AS steam_cycl_bspd,
    SUM(f.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty) AS water_inj_bwpd,
    MAX(f.avg_flw_line_temp_qty) AS flt_f,
    SUM(f.prod_uptm_days_qty) AS uptime_days
FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact f
WHERE f.cmpl_dmn_key IN (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_dmn_key
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    WHERE cd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
)
  AND f.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -180)
GROUP BY f.eftv_dttm
ORDER BY f.eftv_dttm

SELECT
    cdf.eftv_dttm,
    cdf.aloc_oil_prod_vol_qty AS oil_vol,
    cdf.aloc_gros_prod_vol_qty AS gross_vol,
    cdf.aloc_wtr_prod_vol_qty AS water_vol,
    cdf.prod_uptm_secs_qty,
    ROUND(cdf.prod_uptm_secs_qty / 3600, 1) AS runtime_hrs,
    cdf.aloc_stm_inj_vol_qty AS steam_inj_vol,
    cdf.aloc_cnts_stm_inj_vol_qty AS steam_cnts_vol,
    cdf.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_vol_qty AS steam_cycl_vol,
    cdf.aloc_wtr_inj_vol_qty AS water_inj_vol,
    cdf.tgt_eng_stm_inj_vol_qty AS eng_target_steam,
    cdf.tgt_opns_stm_inj_vol_qty AS ops_target_steam,
    cdf.tgt_eng_wtr_inj_vol_qty AS eng_target_water,
    cdf.tgt_opns_wtr_inj_vol_qty AS ops_target_water,
    cdf.flow_line_temp_qty,
    cdf.wlhd_tbg_prsr_qty AS tbg_press,
    cdf.wlhd_csg_prsr_qty AS csg_press,
    cdf.wlhd_tbg_temp_qty AS tbg_temp,
    cdf.wlhd_csg_temp_qty AS csg_temp,
    cdf.lst_off_rsn_type_cde,
    cdf.lst_opnl_stat_on_indc
FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dly_fact cdf
WHERE cdf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
  AND cdf.eftv_dttm >= :date_range_start
  AND cdf.eftv_dttm <= :date_range_end
ORDER BY cdf.eftv_dttm DESC

SQLstep_17_1_cyclic_steam_daily

Reconstructs CSS cycles from daily cyclic steam volumes — groups injection days into cycles (7-day gap = new cycle), calculates per-cycle start/end, steam days, total and average daily volumes

needscmpl_fac_id
row cap500
only runs whenis_producer
Producer · OR1321H-12

3 row(s)

CYCLE_NBRINJECTION_STARTINJECTION_ENDSTEAM_DAYSTOTAL_STEAM_BBLAVG_DAILY_STEAM_BBL
32026-02-13T00:00:002026-02-26T00:00:001413924995
22025-10-16T00:00:002025-10-24T00:00:0096682742
12022-04-29T00:00:002022-06-02T00:00:003533541958
Injector · OR5580-12

nothing returned for this well

WITH steam_days AS (
    SELECT
        cdf.eftv_dttm,
        cdf.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_vol_qty AS stm_vol,
        LAG(cdf.eftv_dttm) OVER (ORDER BY cdf.eftv_dttm) AS prev_date
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dly_fact cdf
    WHERE cdf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND NVL(cdf.aloc_cycl_stm_inj_vol_qty, 0) > 0
),
cycle_groups AS (
    SELECT
        eftv_dttm, stm_vol,
        SUM(CASE WHEN eftv_dttm - prev_date > 7 OR prev_date IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
            OVER (ORDER BY eftv_dttm) AS cycle_grp
    FROM steam_days
)
SELECT
    cycle_grp AS cycle_nbr,
    MIN(eftv_dttm) AS injection_start,
    MAX(eftv_dttm) AS injection_end,
    COUNT(*) AS steam_days,
    ROUND(SUM(stm_vol), 0) AS total_steam_bbl,
    ROUND(AVG(stm_vol), 0) AS avg_daily_steam_bbl
FROM cycle_groups
GROUP BY cycle_grp
ORDER BY cycle_grp DESC

    )

SQLstep_18e_compliance

Steam / cyclic compliance gate — MIT I & MIT II next-due + last-pass (DWRPTG.CMPL_MIT_RSLT_VW, internal/LEGO dates), DO-NOT-Steam governance flag + reason (PUB_FAC_CLS_VW), and the WellStar regulatory well type (CMPL_OIL_GAS_ACCT_DMN)

needscmpl_fac_id, well_fac_id
row cap500
Producer · OR1321H-12

1 row(s)

REGULATORY_WELL_TYPEMITI_NEXT_DUEMITII_NEXT_DUEMITI_LAST_PASSMITII_LAST_PASSMITI_OVERDUEMITII_OVERDUEDO_NOT_STEAMDNS_REASON
OG2027-10-24T00:00:0010/24/2025 07:20:00NNN
Injector · OR5580-12

1 row(s)

REGULATORY_WELL_TYPEMITI_NEXT_DUEMITII_NEXT_DUEMITI_LAST_PASSMITII_LAST_PASSMITI_OVERDUEMITII_OVERDUEDO_NOT_STEAMDNS_REASON
SF2030-12-31T00:00:0012/31/2025 00:00:00NNN
WITH mit AS (
    SELECT cmpl_fac_id,
           MAX(CASE WHEN mit_tst_type_cde = 'MIT1' THEN next_lego_mit_dte   END) AS miti_next_due,
           MAX(CASE WHEN mit_tst_type_cde = 'MIT2' THEN next_lego_mit_dte   END) AS mitii_next_due,
           MAX(CASE WHEN mit_tst_type_cde = 'MIT1' THEN ltst_lego_pass_dttm END) AS miti_last_pass,
           MAX(CASE WHEN mit_tst_type_cde = 'MIT2' THEN ltst_lego_pass_dttm END) AS mitii_last_pass
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mit_rslt_vw
    WHERE cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
    GROUP BY cmpl_fac_id
),
dns AS (
    SELECT CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 0 THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS do_not_steam,
           MAX(pf.lvl3_pub_grp_nme) AS dns_reason
    FROM dwrptg.pub_fac_cls_vw pf
    WHERE pf.lvl1_pub_grp_nme = 'DO NOT Steam'
      AND pf.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
),
wellstar AS (
    SELECT MAX(rgry_well_type_cde) AS rgry_well_type_cde
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_oil_gas_acct_dmn
    WHERE cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND actv_indc = 'Y'
)
SELECT
    ws.rgry_well_type_cde AS regulatory_well_type,
    m.miti_next_due,
    m.mitii_next_due,
    m.miti_last_pass,
    m.mitii_last_pass,
    CASE WHEN m.miti_next_due  IS NOT NULL AND m.miti_next_due  < TRUNC(SYSDATE) THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS miti_overdue,
    CASE WHEN m.mitii_next_due IS NOT NULL AND m.mitii_next_due < TRUNC(SYSDATE) THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS mitii_overdue,
    d.do_not_steam,
    d.dns_reason
FROM (SELECT 1 AS one FROM dual) base
LEFT JOIN wellstar ws ON 1 = 1
LEFT JOIN mit m        ON 1 = 1
LEFT JOIN dns d        ON 1 = 1

    E.EVENT_OBJECTIVE_2 AS reason_code,
    E.DATE_OPS_START AS event_start,
    E.DATE_OPS_END AS event_end,
    ROUND(E.DATE_OPS_END - E.DATE_OPS_START) AS duration_days
FROM EDMADMIN.DM_EVENT_T E
JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON E.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND NVL(E.STATUS_END, 'none') != 'CAN'
  AND E.DATE_OPS_END IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY E.DATE_OPS_END

Monthly load span trending with production overlay — computes load span (max-min rod load) averaged per month, alongside gross/oil production and fillage. Flags each month as PUMP_OK, PUMP_DEGRADATION, or NO_DATA based on load span trajectory vs 6-month prior

2022-09-01T00:00:0025543311622428100928124.12364.4NO_BASELINENO_DATA
Injector · OR5580-12

WITH monthly_loads AS (
    SELECT
        TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM') AS month_dt,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_max_rod_load), 0) AS avg_max_load_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_min_rod_load), 0) AS avg_min_load_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_max_rod_load - pc.dly_min_rod_load), 0) AS avg_load_span_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_avg_pmp_flge), 1) AS avg_fillage_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.bpmp_spm), 1) AS avg_spm,
        COUNT(*) AS card_count
    FROM dwrptg.dly_poc_card_anal_fact pc
    WHERE pc.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND pc.dly_max_rod_load IS NOT NULL
      AND pc.dly_min_rod_load IS NOT NULL
      AND pc.dly_max_rod_load > pc.dly_min_rod_load
    GROUP BY TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM')
),
monthly_prod AS (
    SELECT
        cmf.eftv_dttm AS month_dt,
        ROUND(cmf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS oil_bopd,
        ROUND(cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS gross_bfpd
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact cmf
    WHERE cmf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
)
SELECT
    ml.month_dt,
    ml.avg_max_load_lb,
    ml.avg_min_load_lb,
    ml.avg_load_span_lb,
    LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) AS load_span_6mo_ago,
    ml.avg_fillage_pct,
    ml.avg_spm,
    ml.card_count,
    mp.oil_bopd,
    mp.gross_bfpd,
    LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) AS gross_6mo_ago,
    CASE
        WHEN LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) IS NULL THEN 'NO_BASELINE'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
        THEN 'NARROWING'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb > LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 1.15
        THEN 'WIDENING'
        ELSE 'STABLE'
    END AS load_span_trend,
    CASE
        WHEN LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) IS NULL THEN 'NO_DATA'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb >= LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd < LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'RESERVOIR_DECLINE_NOT_PUMP'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd < LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'PUMP_DEGRADATION'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd >= LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'PUMP_WEAKENING_PROD_OK'
        ELSE 'PUMP_OK'
    END AS diagnostic_flag
FROM monthly_loads ml
LEFT JOIN monthly_prod mp ON ml.month_dt = mp.month_dt
ORDER BY ml.month_dt

    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4121 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS max_rod_load_lb,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4122 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS min_rod_load_lb,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4945 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS avg_csg_prsr_psig,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 3856 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS max_csg_prsr_psig,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4947 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS min_csg_prsr_psig,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4944 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS avg_flowline_temp_f,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 3357 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS max_flowline_temp_f,
    MAX(CASE WHEN pt.parm_type_id = 4946 THEN TO_NUMBER(fdp.nrml_valu_acqn_valu) END) AS min_flowline_temp_f
FROM dwrptg.fac_dyn_parm_dly_fact fdp
JOIN dwrptg.parm_type_dmn pt ON fdp.parm_type_dmn_key = pt.parm_type_dmn_key
WHERE fdp.fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
  AND fdp.eftv_dttm >= :date_range_start
GROUP BY fdp.eftv_dttm
ORDER BY fdp.eftv_dttm DESC

SQLstep_15b_pump_param_changes

Unified timeline of pump parameter changes — SPM, stroke length, and pump depth change events from XSPOC card history (one row per change, not daily), plus pump bore/diameter changes from assembly install history

needscmpl_fac_id, well_fac_id
row cap500
only runs whenis_producer
Producer · OR1321H-12

122 row(s), first 3 shown

CHANGE_DATECHANGE_TYPEOLD_VALUENEW_VALUEUNITCURRENT_SPMCURRENT_STROKECURRENT_DEPTH
2022-07-07T00:00:00SPM98spm82143018
2022-07-08T00:00:00SPM89spm92143018
2022-08-20T00:00:00SPM98spm82143018
Injector · OR5580-12

nothing returned for this well

WITH daily_params AS (
    SELECT
        pc.eftv_dttm,
        ROUND(pc.bpmp_spm) AS spm,
        ROUND(pc.surf_bpmp_strk_lgth) AS stroke_in,
        ROUND(pc.pump_dpth) AS pump_depth_ft,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm) ORDER BY pc.eftv_dttm DESC) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.dly_poc_card_anal_fact pc
    WHERE pc.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND pc.bpmp_spm > 0
),
daily_dedup AS (
    SELECT eftv_dttm, spm, stroke_in, pump_depth_ft
    FROM daily_params WHERE rn = 1
),
with_prev AS (
    SELECT
        eftv_dttm,
        spm, stroke_in, pump_depth_ft,
        LAG(spm) OVER (ORDER BY eftv_dttm) AS prev_spm,
        LAG(stroke_in) OVER (ORDER BY eftv_dttm) AS prev_stroke,
        LAG(pump_depth_ft) OVER (ORDER BY eftv_dttm) AS prev_depth
    FROM daily_dedup
),
xspoc_changes AS (
    SELECT
        eftv_dttm AS change_date,
        CASE
            WHEN spm != NVL(prev_spm, spm) THEN 'SPM'
            WHEN stroke_in != NVL(prev_stroke, stroke_in) THEN 'STROKE_LENGTH'
            WHEN pump_depth_ft != NVL(prev_depth, pump_depth_ft) THEN 'PUMP_DEPTH'
        END AS change_type,
        CASE
            WHEN spm != NVL(prev_spm, spm) THEN TO_CHAR(prev_spm)
            WHEN stroke_in != NVL(prev_stroke, stroke_in) THEN TO_CHAR(prev_stroke)
            WHEN pump_depth_ft != NVL(prev_depth, pump_depth_ft) THEN TO_CHAR(prev_depth)
        END AS old_value,
        CASE
            WHEN spm != NVL(prev_spm, spm) THEN TO_CHAR(spm)
            WHEN stroke_in != NVL(prev_stroke, stroke_in) THEN TO_CHAR(stroke_in)
            WHEN pump_depth_ft != NVL(prev_depth, pump_depth_ft) THEN TO_CHAR(pump_depth_ft)
        END AS new_value,
        CASE
            WHEN spm != NVL(prev_spm, spm) THEN 'spm'
            WHEN stroke_in != NVL(prev_stroke, stroke_in) THEN 'in'
            WHEN pump_depth_ft != NVL(prev_depth, pump_depth_ft) THEN 'ft'
        END AS unit,
        spm AS current_spm,
        stroke_in AS current_stroke,
        pump_depth_ft AS current_depth
    FROM with_prev
    WHERE spm != NVL(prev_spm, spm)
       OR stroke_in != NVL(prev_stroke, stroke_in)
       OR (pump_depth_ft != NVL(prev_depth, pump_depth_ft)
           AND pump_depth_ft > 0 AND NVL(prev_depth, 0) > 0)
),
pump_installs AS (
    SELECT
        wa.nstl_dttm AS change_date,
        'PUMP_BORE' AS change_type,
        LAG(TO_CHAR(wa.nmnl_od_qty)) OVER (ORDER BY wa.nstl_dttm) AS old_value,
        TO_CHAR(wa.nmnl_od_qty) AS new_value,
        'in' AS unit,
        NULL AS current_spm,
        NULL AS current_stroke,
        ROUND(wa.btm_md_qty) AS current_depth
    FROM dwrptg.wlbr_asbly_dmn wa
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON wa.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND wa.wlbr_asbly_cls_desc = 'Conventional Pump'
)
SELECT change_date, change_type, old_value, new_value, unit,
       current_spm, current_stroke, current_depth
FROM xspoc_changes
UNION ALL
SELECT change_date, change_type, old_value, new_value, unit,
       current_spm, current_stroke, current_depth
FROM pump_installs
WHERE old_value IS NOT NULL
   AND old_value != new_value
ORDER BY change_date

SELECT
    wa.wlbr_asbly_cls_desc AS assembly_class,
    wa.wlbr_asbly_type_desc AS assembly_type,
    wa.nmnl_od_qty AS od_inches,
    wa.top_md_qty,
    wa.btm_md_qty,
    wa.nstl_dttm AS install_date,
    ROUND(SYSDATE - wa.nstl_dttm) AS days_in_hole,
    wa.top_lthsg_unit_nme AS formation_top,
    wa.btm_lthsg_unit_nme AS formation_btm,
    wa.parl_indc AS parallel_flag
FROM dwrptg.wlbr_asbly_dmn wa
JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON wa.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
  AND wa.pull_dttm IS NULL
ORDER BY
    CASE wa.wlbr_asbly_cls_desc
        WHEN 'CONDUCTOR CASING' THEN 1
        WHEN 'SURFACE CASING' THEN 2
        WHEN 'PRODUCTION CASING' THEN 3
        WHEN 'PRODUCTION LINER' THEN 4
        WHEN 'TUBING' THEN 5
        WHEN 'ROD STRING' THEN 6
        WHEN 'Conventional Pump' THEN 7
        WHEN 'PLUG' THEN 8
        ELSE 9
    END,
    wa.top_md_qty NULLS LAST

WITH TIGHT AS (
    SELECT TIGHT_GROUP_ID FROM EDMADMIN.MD_SITE_TIGHT_GROUP
    WHERE UPPER(TIGHT_GROUP_NAME) = 'UNRESTRICTED'
),
TIGHT_WELLS AS (
    SELECT WELL_ID FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE
    WHERE TIGHT_GROUP_ID IN (SELECT TIGHT_GROUP_ID FROM TIGHT)
),
WELL_DATUM AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE WELL_ID, DATUM_ELEVATION
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_DATUM_T WHERE IS_DEFAULT = 'Y'
),
PULLED AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE PUMP.WELL_ID, PUMP.WELLBORE_ID, PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID,
           STAT.DATE_STATUS AS TERM_DTTM
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WEQP_CPUMP_T PUMP
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_STATUS_T STAT
        ON PUMP.WELL_ID = STAT.WELL_ID
        AND PUMP.WELLBORE_ID = STAT.WELLBORE_ID
        AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID = STAT.ASSEMBLY_ID
        AND STAT.STATUS = 'PULLED'
),
INSTALL AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE PUMP.WELL_ID, PUMP.WELLBORE_ID, PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID,
           PUMP.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID, STAT.DATE_STATUS AS EFTV_DTTM
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WEQP_CPUMP_T PUMP
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_STATUS_T STAT
        ON PUMP.WELL_ID = STAT.WELL_ID
        AND PUMP.WELLBORE_ID = STAT.WELLBORE_ID
        AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID = STAT.ASSEMBLY_ID
        AND STAT.STATUS = 'INSTALLED'
),
PUMP_STAT AS (
    SELECT I.WELL_ID, I.WELLBORE_ID, I.ASSEMBLY_ID, I.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID,
           I.EFTV_DTTM, P.TERM_DTTM
    FROM INSTALL I
    LEFT JOIN PULLED P ON I.WELL_ID = P.WELL_ID
        AND I.WELLBORE_ID = P.WELLBORE_ID
        AND I.ASSEMBLY_ID = P.ASSEMBLY_ID
)
SELECT
    MIN(PS.EFTV_DTTM) AS install_date,
    NULLIF(MAX(COALESCE(PS.TERM_DTTM, TO_DATE('9999-12-31','yyyy-mm-dd'))),
           TO_DATE('9999-12-31','yyyy-mm-dd')) AS pull_date,
    PUMP.API_PUMP_TYPE AS pump_type,
    PUMP.API_PUMP_BORE AS pump_bore,
    PUMP.API_BARREL_TYPE AS barrel_type_code,
    PUMP.BARREL_TYPE AS barrel_type_desc,
    PUMP.API_BARREL_LENGTH AS barrel_length,
    PUMP.PLUNGER_TYPE AS plunger_type_code,
    PUMP.API_TBG_SIZE_CODE AS tubing_size,
    PUMP.API_SEATING_ASS_LOC AS seating_location,
    PUMP.API_TYPE_SEAT_ASS AS seating_type,
    COMP.DESCRIPTION AS pump_description,
    COMP.INTERNAL_REF AS pump_serial_no,
    COMP.SUPPLIER AS supplier,
    COMP.SERVICE_TYPE AS service_feature,
    ROUND(COMP.TOP_CONN_LENGTH, 2) AS pump_depth,
    ROUND(COMP.BOT_CONN_LENGTH, 2) AS pump_shoe_depth
FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WEQP_CPUMP_T PUMP
JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_COMP_T COMP
    ON PUMP.WELL_ID = COMP.WELL_ID
    AND PUMP.WELLBORE_ID = COMP.WELLBORE_ID
    AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID = COMP.ASSEMBLY_ID
    AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID = COMP.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID
JOIN PUMP_STAT PS ON PUMP.WELL_ID = PS.WELL_ID
    AND PUMP.WELLBORE_ID = PS.WELLBORE_ID
    AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_ID = PS.ASSEMBLY_ID
    AND PUMP.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID = PS.ASSEMBLY_COMP_ID
JOIN WELL_DATUM WD ON PUMP.WELL_ID = WD.WELL_ID
JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON PUMP.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND PUMP.WELL_ID IN (SELECT WELL_ID FROM TIGHT_WELLS)
GROUP BY PUMP.API_PUMP_TYPE, PUMP.API_PUMP_BORE, PUMP.API_BARREL_TYPE,
         PUMP.BARREL_TYPE, PUMP.API_BARREL_LENGTH, PUMP.PLUNGER_TYPE,
         PUMP.API_TBG_SIZE_CODE, PUMP.API_SEATING_ASS_LOC, PUMP.API_TYPE_SEAT_ASS,
         COMP.DESCRIPTION, COMP.INTERNAL_REF, COMP.SUPPLIER,
         COMP.SERVICE_TYPE, COMP.TOP_CONN_LENGTH, COMP.BOT_CONN_LENGTH
ORDER BY install_date DESC

WITH WELL_DATUM AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE WELL_ID, DATUM_ELEVATION
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_DATUM_T WHERE IS_DEFAULT = 'Y'
),
STATUS AS (
    SELECT WELL_ID, WELLBORE_ID, ASSEMBLY_ID, STATUS, DATE_STATUS,
           CASE WHEN STATUS = 'PULLED' THEN DATE_STATUS END AS PULL_DTE,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY WELL_ID, WELLBORE_ID, ASSEMBLY_ID
                              ORDER BY DATE_STATUS DESC) AS RN
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_STATUS_T
)
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
        C.STRING_TYPE,
        I.DESCRIPTION AS comp_type_desc,
        D.COMP_NAME AS comp_name,
        D.DESCRIPTION AS item_description,
        D.OD_BODY AS od_inches,
        D.NOMINAL_WEIGHT AS weight_ppf,
        D.GRADE,
        D.CONNECTION_TYPE AS threads,
        ROUND(D.MD_TOP + H.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS top_tmd,
        ROUND(D.MD_BASE + H.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS base_tmd,
        D.LENGTH AS item_length,
        D.INTERNAL_REF AS serial_number,
        D.SUPPLIER,
        D.MODEL,
        D.CONDITION AS condition,
        D.REMARKS AS item_detail
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE A
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELLBORE_T B ON A.WELL_ID = B.WELL_ID
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_T C ON C.WELLBORE_ID = B.WELLBORE_ID AND C.WELL_ID = A.WELL_ID
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_ASSEMBLY_COMP_T D
        ON D.ASSEMBLY_ID = C.ASSEMBLY_ID AND D.WELL_ID = C.WELL_ID AND D.WELLBORE_ID = C.WELLBORE_ID
    JOIN STATUS E
        ON E.ASSEMBLY_ID = C.ASSEMBLY_ID AND C.WELL_ID = E.WELL_ID
        AND E.WELLBORE_ID = C.WELLBORE_ID AND E.PULL_DTE IS NULL
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID F ON A.WELL_ID = F.WELL_ID
    JOIN WELL_DATUM H ON A.WELL_ID = H.WELL_ID
    JOIN EDMADMIN.MD_PK_COMP_TYPE I ON I.COMP_TYPE_CODE = D.COMP_TYPE_CODE AND I.SECT_TYPE_CODE = D.SECT_TYPE_CODE
    WHERE F.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
      AND D.COMP_TYPE_CODE != 'PKRE'
    ORDER BY C.STRING_TYPE, D.MD_TOP
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 200

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT
        awo.actl_opg_ntvl_type_desc AS interval_type,
        awo.top_md_qty,
        awo.btm_md_qty,
        awo.top_tvd_qty,
        awo.btm_tvd_qty,
        ROUND(awo.btm_md_qty - awo.top_md_qty, 1) AS interval_length_ft,
        awo.cmpl_eftv_dttm AS effective_date,
        awo.cmpl_term_dttm AS termination_date,
        CASE
            WHEN awo.wlbr_opg_clo_mthd_desc IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
            WHEN awo.cmpl_term_dttm IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
            ELSE 'OPEN'
        END AS status,
        awo.slot_width_qty,
        awo.slot_lgth_qty,
        awo.slot_dens_qty AS slots_per_ft,
        awo.slot_spac_qty,
        awo.perf_diam_qty AS perf_diameter_in,
        awo.perf_dens_qty AS shots_per_foot,
        awo.perf_phsg_deg_type_desc AS perf_phasing,
        awo.perf_opg_purp_type_desc AS perf_purpose,
        awo.perf_pre_drld_indc AS pre_drilled,
        awo.wlbr_opg_clo_mthd_desc AS closure_method,
        awo.wlbr_opg_ntvl_stat_eftv_dttm AS status_effective_date,
        awo.top_lthsg_unit_nme AS formation_top,
        awo.btm_lthsg_unit_nme AS formation_btm,
        awo.actv_indc,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY awo.top_md_qty, awo.btm_md_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN awo.wlbr_opg_clo_mthd_desc IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
                    WHEN awo.cmpl_term_dttm IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
                    ELSE 'OPEN'
                END
            ORDER BY awo.cmpl_eftv_dttm DESC
        ) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn awo
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON awo.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND awo.actv_indc = 'Y'
)
SELECT interval_type, top_md_qty, btm_md_qty, top_tvd_qty, btm_tvd_qty,
       interval_length_ft, effective_date, termination_date, status,
       slot_width_qty, slot_lgth_qty, slots_per_ft, slot_spac_qty,
       perf_diameter_in, shots_per_foot, perf_phasing, perf_purpose,
       pre_drilled, closure_method, status_effective_date,
       formation_top, formation_btm, actv_indc
FROM ranked
WHERE rn = 1
ORDER BY effective_date, top_md_qty
SQLstep_8b_completion_geology_overlay

Joins open perforations against formation tops — calculates open footage per geologic zone, with zone thickness, perforation status and type

needswell_fac_id
row cap500
Producer · OR1321H-12

nothing returned for this well

Injector · OR5580-12

27 row(s), first 3 shown

FORMATIONFORMATION_TOP_MDFORMATION_BTM_MDZONE_THICKNESS_FTHAS_OPEN_PERFSOPEN_FEET_IN_ZONEINTERVAL_TYPEPERF_STATUS
BFW1422.81564.1141.3NO
BPR1564.11717.9153.8NO
TSM1717.91828110.1NO
WITH perfs AS (
    SELECT
        ow.top_md_qty AS perf_top,
        ow.btm_md_qty AS perf_btm,
        ow.actl_opg_ntvl_type_desc AS interval_type,
        ow.cmpl_eftv_dttm AS open_date,
        CASE
            WHEN ow.wlbr_opg_clo_mthd_desc IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
            WHEN ow.cmpl_term_dttm IS NOT NULL THEN 'CLOSED'
            ELSE 'OPEN'
        END AS status
    FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn ow
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON ow.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND ow.actv_indc = 'Y'
),
tops AS (
    SELECT
        mp.mrkr_nme,
        mp.md_qty AS top_md,
        mp.tvd_qty AS top_tvd,
        LEAD(mp.md_qty) OVER (ORDER BY mp.md_qty) AS next_top_md,
        LEAD(mp.mrkr_nme) OVER (ORDER BY mp.md_qty) AS next_marker
    FROM dwrptg.wlbr_mrkr_pick_dmn mp
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON mp.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND mp.term_dttm IS NULL
)
SELECT
    t.mrkr_nme AS formation,
    ROUND(t.top_md, 1) AS formation_top_md,
    ROUND(t.next_top_md, 1) AS formation_btm_md,
    ROUND(t.next_top_md - t.top_md, 1) AS zone_thickness_ft,
    CASE
        WHEN p.perf_top IS NOT NULL THEN 'YES'
        ELSE 'NO'
    END AS has_open_perfs,
    CASE
        WHEN p.perf_top IS NOT NULL THEN
            ROUND(LEAST(p.perf_btm, NVL(t.next_top_md, p.perf_btm))
                - GREATEST(p.perf_top, t.top_md), 1)
        ELSE NULL
    END AS open_feet_in_zone,
    p.interval_type,
    p.status AS perf_status
FROM tops t
LEFT JOIN perfs p
    ON p.perf_btm > t.top_md
    AND p.perf_top < NVL(t.next_top_md, 99999)
    AND p.status = 'OPEN'
WHERE t.next_top_md IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY t.top_md

        D.MD_QTY AS survey_md,
        D.TVD_QTY AS survey_tvd,

    SELECT
        D.MD_QTY AS survey_md,
        D.TVD_QTY AS survey_tvd,
        D.VERT_DVTN_QTY AS inclination,
        D.AZIM_QTY AS azimuth,
        D.DGLG_SVR_QTY AS dogleg_severity
    FROM DWRPTG.DSVY_PT_DMN D
    JOIN DWRPTG.WLBR_DMN B ON D.WLBR_FAC_ID = B.WLBR_FAC_ID
    WHERE B.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
    ORDER BY D.MD_QTY DESC
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
)
ORDER BY 1

    ROUND(CFS.MD_TOP, 1) AS stage_top_md,
    ROUND(CFS.MD_BASE, 1) AS stage_btm_md,

WITH TIGHT AS (
    SELECT TIGHT_GROUP_ID FROM EDMADMIN.MD_SITE_TIGHT_GROUP
    WHERE UPPER(TIGHT_GROUP_NAME) = 'UNRESTRICTED'
),
TIGHT_WELLS AS (
    SELECT WELL_ID FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE
    WHERE TIGHT_GROUP_ID IN (SELECT TIGHT_GROUP_ID FROM TIGHT)
)
SELECT
    WR.DATE_EFFECTIVE AS note_date,
    WR.REMARKS_TYPE AS note_type,
    CAST(WR.WELL_REMARKS AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS remarks,
    W.WELL_COMMON_NAME AS well_name
FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_REMARKS_T WR
JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE W ON WR.WELL_ID = W.WELL_ID
JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON WR.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND WR.WELL_ID IN (SELECT WELL_ID FROM TIGHT_WELLS)
  AND WR.DATE_EFFECTIVE IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY WR.DATE_EFFECTIVE DESC

SQLstep_9b_wellbore_obstructions

Current (non-pulled) wellbore obstructions from OpenWells — string type, obstruction type, date, depths (TMD), status, install date, comments

needswell_fac_id
row cap500
Producer · OR1321H-12

nothing returned for this well

Injector · OR5580-12

2 row(s)

STRING_TYPEOBSTRUCTION_TYPEOBSTRUCTION_DATEOBSTRUCTION_TOP_TMDOBSTRUCTION_BASE_TMDSTATUSINSTALLED_DTTMCOMMENTS
CasingDAMAGED CSG.2020-03-02T00:00:002170INSTALLED2020-03-02T00:00:00Crimped Connection at the collar
CasingINSTALLEDPOS/AW scrapers stopped
WITH WELL_DATUM AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE WELL_ID, DATUM_ELEVATION
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_DATUM_T WHERE IS_DEFAULT = 'Y'
),
PULLED_OBS AS (
    SELECT A.WELL_ID, A.WELLBORE_ID, A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID,
           B.DATE_STATUS AS PULLED_DTTM
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_WELLBORE_OBSTRUCTION_T A
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_WB_OBSTRUCTION_STATUS_T B
        ON A.WELL_ID = B.WELL_ID AND A.WELLBORE_ID = B.WELLBORE_ID
        AND A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID = B.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID
    WHERE B.STATUS = 'PULLED'
),
INSTALLED_OBS AS (
    SELECT A.WELL_ID, A.WELLBORE_ID, A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID,
           A.STRING_TYPE, A.WELLBORE_TOP_MD, A.WELLBORE_BASE_MD,
           A.OBSTRUCTION_DATE, A.OBSTRUCTION_TYPE, A.COMMENTS,
           B.STATUS, B.DATE_STATUS AS INSTALLED_DTTM,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY A.WELL_ID, A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID
                              ORDER BY B.DATE_STATUS DESC) AS R_NUM
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_WELLBORE_OBSTRUCTION_T A
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_WB_OBSTRUCTION_STATUS_T B
        ON A.WELL_ID = B.WELL_ID AND A.WELLBORE_ID = B.WELLBORE_ID
        AND A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID = B.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID
    LEFT JOIN PULLED_OBS P
        ON A.WELL_ID = P.WELL_ID AND A.WELLBORE_ID = P.WELLBORE_ID
        AND A.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID = P.WB_OBSTRUCTION_ID
    WHERE P.PULLED_DTTM IS NULL
)
SELECT
    IO.STRING_TYPE,
    IO.OBSTRUCTION_TYPE,
    IO.OBSTRUCTION_DATE,
    ROUND(IO.WELLBORE_TOP_MD + WD.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS obstruction_top_tmd,
    ROUND(IO.WELLBORE_BASE_MD + WD.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS obstruction_base_tmd,
    IO.STATUS,
    IO.INSTALLED_DTTM,
    IO.COMMENTS
FROM INSTALLED_OBS IO
JOIN WELL_DATUM WD ON IO.WELL_ID = WD.WELL_ID
JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON IO.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND IO.R_NUM = 1

Equipment failures from OpenWells — failure date, failed system, failure type/manufacturer, failure depth (TMD), pull cause code, days to failure. Top 50

WITH WELL_DATUM AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE WELL_ID, DATUM_ELEVATION
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_DATUM_T WHERE IS_DEFAULT = 'Y'
)
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
        C.DATE_OPS_END AS failure_date,
        B.FAILED_EQUIP_SYSTEM AS failed_equipment,
        B.JOB_TYPE AS failure_type,
        B.FAILED_EQUIP_MANUFACTURER AS failure_root_cause,
        ROUND(B.FAILURE_MD + WD.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS failure_tmd,
        B.FAILURE_CAUSE AS pull_cause_code,
        B.DAYS_TO_FAILURE
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_PROD_EQUIP_FAIL_T B
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_EVENT_T C ON B.WELL_ID = C.WELL_ID AND B.EVENT_ID = C.EVENT_ID
    JOIN WELL_DATUM WD ON B.WELL_ID = WD.WELL_ID
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON B.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
    ORDER BY C.DATE_OPS_END DESC
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 50
SQLstep_9d_stim_treatments

Stimulation treatments from OpenWells — date, treatment type, interval depths (TMD), zone, frac gradient, proppant lbs, volume pumped. Top 30

needswell_fac_id
row cap500
Producer · OR1321H-12

nothing returned for this well

Injector · OR5580-12

1 row(s)

STIM_DATETREATMENT_TYPEINTERVAL_TOP_TMDINTERVAL_BASE_TMDZONETIME_STARTFRAC_GRADIENT_PSI_FTPROPPANT_LBSVOLUME_PUMPED
2013-11-26T00:00:00BALL OUT TREATMENT224222740
WITH WELL_DATUM AS (
    SELECT UNIQUE WELL_ID, DATUM_ELEVATION
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_DATUM_T WHERE IS_DEFAULT = 'Y'
)
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
        C.DATE_OPS_END AS stim_date,
        B.JOB_TYPE AS treatment_type,
        ROUND(B.INTERVAL_TOP + WD.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS interval_top_tmd,
        ROUND(B.INTERVAL_BASE + WD.DATUM_ELEVATION, 2) AS interval_base_tmd,
        B.ZONE,
        B.TIME_START,
        ROUND(B.INJECT_RATE_AVERAGE * 0.1781075952381, 2) AS frac_gradient_psi_ft,
        B.PROPPANT_IN_FORMATION * 2000 AS proppant_lbs,
        ROUND(B.VOL_PUMPED, 2) AS volume_pumped
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_STIM_TREATMENT_T B
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_STIM_JOB_T D
        ON B.WELL_ID = D.WELL_ID AND B.STIM_JOB_ID = D.STIM_JOB_ID AND D.PHASE = 'ACTUAL'
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_EVENT_T C ON B.WELL_ID = C.WELL_ID AND B.EVENT_ID = C.EVENT_ID
    JOIN WELL_DATUM WD ON B.WELL_ID = WD.WELL_ID
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON B.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
    ORDER BY C.DATE_OPS_END DESC
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 30

SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
        E.DATE_OPS_START AS event_date,
        E.EVENT_ID AS ow_event_id,
        B.HSE_TYPE AS category,
        B.ACTIVITY_PHASE AS qualifier,
        B.DESCRIPTION AS lookback_description,
        A.WELL_COMMON_NAME AS well_name
    FROM EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE A
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_WELL_PLAN_HSE_T B ON A.WELL_ID = B.WELL_ID
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELLBORE_T C ON A.WELL_ID = C.WELL_ID
    JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_WELL_PLAN_T D
        ON A.WELL_ID = D.WELL_ID AND C.WELLBORE_ID = D.WELLBORE_ID
        AND B.WELL_PLAN_ID = D.WELL_PLAN_ID

    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID X ON A.WELL_ID = X.WELL_ID
    WHERE X.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
    ORDER BY E.DATE_OPS_START DESC, B.SEQUENCE_NO ASC
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 20

SELECT
    DAILY.DATE_REPORT AS op_date,
    CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS operational_comment,
    EVENT.EVENT_TYPE AS job_type,
    EVENT.EVENT_OBJECTIVE_1 AS objective,
    EVENT.DATE_OPS_START AS event_start,
    EVENT.DATE_OPS_END AS event_end,

    ON DAILY.WELL_ID = EVENT.WELL_ID AND DAILY.EVENT_ID = EVENT.EVENT_ID

JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON DAILY.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY DAILY.DATE_REPORT DESC

WITH plan_detail AS (
    SELECT
        wp.WELL_PLAN_ID,
        wp.PRODUCING_AREA AS plan_title,
        wp.JOB_TYPE,
        wp.JOB_PRIORITY,
        wp.ESTIMATED_DURATION,
        wp.ACTUAL_DURATION,
        wp.EXPECTED_WORK_START_DATE,
        CAST(wp.WELL_PLAN_NOTE AS VARCHAR2(1000)) AS plan_note,
        wp.AFE_ID,
        op.STEP_NO,
        op.ACTIVITY_CODE,
        op.ACTIVITY_CLASS,
        op.ACTIVITY_DURATION,
        op.MD_FROM,
        op.MD_TO,
        CAST(op.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS planned_procedure,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY wp.WELL_PLAN_ID, op.STEP_NO
            ORDER BY op.WELL_PLAN_OP_ID
        ) AS rn
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_WELL_PLAN_T wp
    LEFT JOIN EDMADMIN.DM_WELL_PLAN_OP_T op
        ON wp.WELL_ID = op.WELL_ID AND wp.WELL_PLAN_ID = op.WELL_PLAN_ID
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON wp.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
)
SELECT plan_title, JOB_TYPE, JOB_PRIORITY, ESTIMATED_DURATION, ACTUAL_DURATION,
       EXPECTED_WORK_START_DATE, plan_note, AFE_ID, STEP_NO, ACTIVITY_CODE,
       ACTIVITY_CLASS, ACTIVITY_DURATION, MD_FROM, MD_TO, planned_procedure
FROM plan_detail
WHERE rn = 1
ORDER BY EXPECTED_WORK_START_DATE DESC NULLS LAST, STEP_NO NULLS LAST

SELECT
    E.EVENT_ID,
    E.EVENT_TYPE,
    E.EVENT_CODE,
    E.EVENT_OBJECTIVE_1 AS failure_reason,
    E.EVENT_OBJECTIVE_2 AS work_description,
    E.EVENT_OBJECTIVE_3,
    E.CREATE_DATE AS planned_date,
    E.DATE_AUTHORIZED,
    E.DATE_OPS_START,
    E.DATE_OPS_END,
    ROUND(E.DATE_OPS_END - E.DATE_OPS_START) AS job_duration_days,
    E.DATE_OFF_PROD,
    E.DATE_ON_PROD,
    E.STATUS_END AS job_end_status,
    E.EQUIP_TYPE,
    CAST(E.REMARKS AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS remarks,
    W.WELL_COMMON_NAME
FROM EDMADMIN.DM_EVENT_T E
JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE W ON E.WELL_ID = W.WELL_ID
JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON E.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
  AND NVL(E.STATUS_END, 'none') != 'CAN'
ORDER BY E.DATE_OPS_END DESC

SELECT * FROM (
    -- (a) per-activity rig line: matched by activity code OR acid chemical in the memo
    SELECT
        a.TIME_FROM,
        a.TIME_TO,
        a.ACTIVITY_DURATION,
        CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS OPERATION_DESCRIPTION,
        a.ACTIVITY_CODE,
        a.ACTIVITY_ALT_CODE2,
        w.WELL_COMMON_NAME,
        'ACTIVITY' AS SOURCE_TYPE
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_ACTIVITY_T a
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE w ON a.WELL_ID = w.WELL_ID
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON a.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
      AND (UPPER(a.ACTIVITY_CODE) IN ('ACID','ACIDDU','ACIDDS','ACIDCW','HPC')
           OR UPPER(a.ACTIVITY_ALT_CODE2) = 'STIM'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%HCL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%HF%')
    UNION ALL
    -- (b) daily operational comment: acid chemical / 'ACID' named in the day's narrative
    SELECT
        DAILY.DATE_REPORT AS TIME_FROM,
        NULL AS TIME_TO,
        NULL AS ACTIVITY_DURATION,
        CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS OPERATION_DESCRIPTION,
        NULL AS ACTIVITY_CODE,
        NULL AS ACTIVITY_ALT_CODE2,
        w.WELL_COMMON_NAME,
        'DAILY_COMMENT' AS SOURCE_TYPE
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_DAILY_T DAILY
    JOIN EDMADMIN.CD_WELL_SOURCE w ON DAILY.WELL_ID = w.WELL_ID
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON DAILY.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
      AND DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL IS NOT NULL
      AND (UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%HCL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%HF%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%ACID%')
    ORDER BY TIME_FROM DESC
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 50

SELECT * FROM (
    -- (a) per-activity rig line
    SELECT
        a.TIME_FROM,
        CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS OPERATION_DESCRIPTION,
        a.ACTIVITY_CODE,
        'ACTIVITY' AS SOURCE_TYPE
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_ACTIVITY_T a
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON a.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
      AND (UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%BAIL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%SCALE%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%TAG%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%SCRAP%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%FILL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(a.ACTIVITY_MEMO AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%DEPOSIT%')
    UNION ALL
    -- (b) daily operational comment narrative
    SELECT
        DAILY.DATE_REPORT AS TIME_FROM,
        CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000)) AS OPERATION_DESCRIPTION,
        NULL AS ACTIVITY_CODE,
        'DAILY_COMMENT' AS SOURCE_TYPE
    FROM EDMADMIN.DM_DAILY_T DAILY
    JOIN TRLN.EDM_2_EDS_WELL_FAC_ID EDS ON DAILY.WELL_ID = EDS.WELL_ID
    WHERE EDS.WELL_FAC_ID = :well_fac_id
      AND DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL IS NOT NULL
      AND (UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%BAIL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%SCALE%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%TAG%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%SCRAP%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%FILL%'
           OR UPPER(CAST(DAILY.COMMENT_OPERATIONAL AS VARCHAR2(2000))) LIKE '%DEPOSIT%')
    ORDER BY TIME_FROM DESC
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 100
SQLstep_19c_decline_vs_pump

36-month monthly production + POC pump (fillage/SPM/stroke) + off-status, PLUS a well-level acid-screen decline summary repeated on each row: YoY% (avg gross last-12 vs prior-12), log-linear slope acceleration (recent-12 vs lifetime ln-gross OLS), oil YoY, annualized decline, lifetime/peak. decline_flag = STEEP_DECLINE / SEVERE_YOY_DROP / OIL_DECLINE_STABLE_GROSS / NORMAL / NO_DATA; pump_status = PUMP_OK / LOW_FILLAGE

WITH life AS (
    SELECT
        cmf.eftv_dttm,
        cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty AS gross_bfpd,
        cmf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty  AS oil_bopd,
        cmf.prod_wtr_cut_pct           AS wc_pct,
        MONTHS_BETWEEN(cmf.eftv_dttm, MIN(cmf.eftv_dttm) OVER ()) AS mon_idx
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact cmf
    WHERE cmf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
),
lifetime AS (
    SELECT
        COUNT(*)                  AS lifetime_months,
        ROUND(AVG(gross_bfpd), 1) AS lifetime_avg,
        ROUND(MAX(gross_bfpd), 1) AS peak_bfpd,
        ROUND(
          (COUNT(*) * SUM(mon_idx * LN(NULLIF(gross_bfpd, 0)))
            - SUM(mon_idx) * SUM(LN(NULLIF(gross_bfpd, 0))))
          / NULLIF(COUNT(*) * SUM(mon_idx * mon_idx) - SUM(mon_idx) * SUM(mon_idx), 0)
        , 6)                      AS lt_slope
    FROM life
),
recent AS (
    SELECT
        ROUND(AVG(gross_bfpd), 1) AS recent_avg,
        ROUND(AVG(oil_bopd), 1)   AS recent_oil_avg,
        ROUND(
          (COUNT(*) * SUM(mon_idx * LN(NULLIF(gross_bfpd, 0)))
            - SUM(mon_idx) * SUM(LN(NULLIF(gross_bfpd, 0))))
          / NULLIF(COUNT(*) * SUM(mon_idx * mon_idx) - SUM(mon_idx) * SUM(mon_idx), 0)
        , 6)                      AS rec_slope
    FROM life
    WHERE eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -12)
),
prior_window AS (
    SELECT
        ROUND(AVG(gross_bfpd), 1) AS prior_avg,
        ROUND(AVG(oil_bopd), 1)   AS prior_oil_avg
    FROM life
    WHERE eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -24)
      AND eftv_dttm <  ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -12)
),
oil_6m AS (
    SELECT ROUND(AVG(oil_bopd), 1) AS oil_6mo_avg_bopd
    FROM life
    WHERE eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -6)
),
pump_data AS (
    SELECT
        TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM') AS month_dt,
        AVG(pc.dly_avg_pmp_flge)    AS avg_fillage,
        AVG(pc.bpmp_spm)            AS avg_spm,
        AVG(pc.surf_bpmp_strk_lgth) AS avg_stroke
    FROM dwrptg.dly_poc_card_anal_fact pc
    WHERE pc.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND pc.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -36)
    GROUP BY TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM')
),
recent_fillage AS (
    SELECT ROUND(AVG(avg_fillage), 1) AS recent_avg_fillage
    FROM pump_data
    WHERE month_dt >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -12)
),
status_months AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT TRUNC(cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm, 'MM') AS off_month
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_opnl_stat_fact cosf
    WHERE cosf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND cosf.opnl_stat_on_indc = 'N'
      AND cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -36)
      AND NVL(cosf.opnl_stat_term_dttm, SYSDATE) - cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm > 7
),
summary AS (
    SELECT
        l.lifetime_months, l.lifetime_avg, l.peak_bfpd,
        r.recent_avg, pw.prior_avg, o6.oil_6mo_avg_bopd, rf.recent_avg_fillage,
        ROUND((r.recent_avg - pw.prior_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_avg, 0) * 100, 1)              AS yoy_pct,
        ROUND((r.recent_oil_avg - pw.prior_oil_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_oil_avg, 0) * 100, 1)  AS oil_yoy_pct,
        ROUND(r.rec_slope / NULLIF(l.lt_slope, 0), 2)                                        AS slope_accel,
        ROUND(r.rec_slope * 12 * 100, 1)                                                     AS ann_decline_pct,
        CASE WHEN NVL(rf.recent_avg_fillage, 50) > 20 THEN 'PUMP_OK' ELSE 'LOW_FILLAGE' END  AS pump_status,
        CASE
            WHEN r.recent_avg IS NULL OR pw.prior_avg IS NULL THEN 'NO_DATA'
            WHEN (r.recent_avg - pw.prior_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_avg, 0) * 100 <= -15
                 AND r.rec_slope < l.lt_slope - 0.001 THEN 'STEEP_DECLINE'
            WHEN (r.recent_avg - pw.prior_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_avg, 0) * 100 <= -30 THEN 'SEVERE_YOY_DROP'
            WHEN (r.recent_avg - pw.prior_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_avg, 0) * 100 > -15
                 AND (r.recent_oil_avg - pw.prior_oil_avg) / NULLIF(pw.prior_oil_avg, 0) * 100 <= -20
                 THEN 'OIL_DECLINE_STABLE_GROSS'
            ELSE 'NORMAL'
        END AS decline_flag
    FROM lifetime l, recent r, prior_window pw, oil_6m o6, recent_fillage rf
)
SELECT
    m.eftv_dttm,
    m.gross_bfpd AS bfpd, m.oil_bopd AS bopd, m.wc_pct,
    p.avg_fillage, p.avg_spm, p.avg_stroke,
    CASE WHEN s.off_month IS NOT NULL THEN 'OFF_PERIOD' ELSE NULL END AS operational_outage,
    su.yoy_pct, su.oil_yoy_pct, su.slope_accel, su.ann_decline_pct,
    su.recent_avg, su.prior_avg, su.lifetime_avg, su.peak_bfpd,
    su.oil_6mo_avg_bopd, su.recent_avg_fillage, su.lifetime_months,
    su.pump_status, su.decline_flag
FROM life m
LEFT JOIN pump_data p     ON m.eftv_dttm = p.month_dt
LEFT JOIN status_months s ON m.eftv_dttm = s.off_month
CROSS JOIN summary su
WHERE m.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -36)
ORDER BY m.eftv_dttm
SQLstep_20_cumulative_production

2021-08-01T00:00:000.00.00.05825197.410.00.00.00.0

SELECT
    dcm.eftv_dttm,
    dcm.aloc_cum_oil_prod_vol_qty AS cum_oil_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_cum_gros_prod_vol_qty AS cum_gross_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_cum_wtr_prod_vol_qty AS cum_water_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_cum_stm_inj_vol_qty AS cum_steam_inj_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_cum_wtr_inj_vol_qty AS cum_water_inj_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_cum_cycl_stm_inj_vol_qty AS cum_cycl_steam_bbl,
    dcm.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty AS oil_bopd,
    dcm.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty AS gross_bfpd,
    CASE WHEN dcm.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
         THEN ROUND(dcm.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty / dcm.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty * 100, 1)
         ELSE NULL END AS water_cut_pct,
    dcm.mon_since_init_prod_qty AS mon_since_init_prod
FROM dss.dss_completion_mnly_fact dcm
WHERE dcm.pid = (
    SELECT pid FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
)
  AND dcm.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -60)
ORDER BY dcm.eftv_dttm

needscmpl_nme, well_fac_id, cmpl_fac_id

OR243-1227390PRODY2242211453190.0225055.013452227.99OVERLAPPINGABND1998-10-01T00:00:00
OR244-1228057PRODY2313151453520.0225038.021272261.99OVERLAPPINGABND
OR238-1212195PRODY2561321453188.0225394.020952254OVERLAPPINGABND
WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
target_intervals AS (
    SELECT MIN(ow.top_md_qty) AS tgt_top_md,
           MAX(ow.btm_md_qty) AS tgt_btm_md
    FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn ow
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON ow.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND ow.actv_indc = 'Y'
      AND ow.cmpl_term_dttm IS NULL
),
ranked_wells AS (
    SELECT
        cd.cmpl_nme,
        cd.cmpl_fac_id,
        cd.well_fac_id,
        cd.prim_purp_type_cde,
        cd.actv_indc,
        cd.in_svc_indc,
        cd.engr_strg_nme,
        cd.cmpl_state_type_cde,
        cm.bottomx,
        cm.bottomy,
        ROUND(SQRT(
            POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
            POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
        )) AS distance_ft,
        ROUND(MOD(ATAN2(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, cm.bottomx - t.bottomx) * 180 / 3.14159265358979 + 360, 360)) AS azimuth_deg,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cd.cmpl_nme ORDER BY cd.actv_indc DESC, cd.cmpl_fac_id DESC) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
)
SELECT * FROM (
    SELECT
        rw.cmpl_nme,
        rw.cmpl_fac_id,
        rw.prim_purp_type_cde,
        rw.actv_indc,
        rw.in_svc_indc,
        rw.engr_strg_nme,
        rw.distance_ft,
        rw.azimuth_deg,
        rw.bottomx,
        rw.bottomy,
        ow_agg.open_top_md AS open_interval_top_md,
        ow_agg.open_btm_md AS open_interval_btm_md,
        CASE
            WHEN ow_agg.open_top_md <= ti.tgt_btm_md
             AND ow_agg.open_btm_md >= ti.tgt_top_md
            THEN 'OVERLAPPING'
            ELSE 'NO_OVERLAP'
        END AS interval_overlap,
        ROUND(imf.curr_oil_bopd, 1) AS curr_oil_bopd,
        ROUND(imf.curr_gross_bfpd, 1) AS curr_gross_bfpd,
        ROUND(imf.curr_water_bwpd, 1) AS curr_water_bwpd,
        ROUND(imf.curr_steam_bspd, 1) AS curr_steam_bspd,
        ROUND(imf.curr_water_inj_bwpd, 1) AS curr_water_inj_bwpd,
        rw.cmpl_state_type_cde AS current_status,
        cosf.opnl_stat_eftv_dttm AS status_since
    FROM ranked_wells rw
    CROSS JOIN target_intervals ti
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT wd2.well_fac_id,
               MIN(ow2.top_md_qty) AS open_top_md,
               MAX(ow2.btm_md_qty) AS open_btm_md
        FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn ow2
        JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd2 ON ow2.wlbr_fac_id = wd2.wlbr_fac_id
        WHERE ow2.actv_indc = 'Y' AND ow2.cmpl_term_dttm IS NULL
        GROUP BY wd2.well_fac_id
    ) ow_agg ON rw.well_fac_id = ow_agg.well_fac_id
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT mf.cmpl_fac_id,
               mf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty AS curr_oil_bopd,
               mf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty AS curr_gross_bfpd,
               mf.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty AS curr_water_bwpd,
               mf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty AS curr_steam_bspd,
               mf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty AS curr_water_inj_bwpd
        FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact mf
        JOIN dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd2 ON mf.cmpl_dmn_key = cd2.cmpl_dmn_key
        WHERE mf.eftv_dttm = ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), CASE WHEN EXTRACT(DAY FROM SYSDATE) >= 10 THEN -1 ELSE -2 END)
    ) imf ON rw.cmpl_fac_id = imf.cmpl_fac_id
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT cmpl_fac_id, MAX(opnl_stat_eftv_dttm) AS opnl_stat_eftv_dttm
        FROM dwrptg.cmpl_opnl_stat_fact
        WHERE opnl_stat_term_dttm IS NULL
        GROUP BY cmpl_fac_id
    ) cosf ON rw.cmpl_fac_id = cosf.cmpl_fac_id
    WHERE rw.rn = 1
    ORDER BY rw.distance_ft
) WHERE ROWNUM <= 50

CMPL_NMECMPL_FAC_IDDISTANCE_FTACTV_INDCIN_SVC_INDCDRILL_DATECUM_OIL_MBBLCUM_GROSS_MBBLMONTHS_PRODUCINGAVG_OIL_BOPD_ONDAYSAVG_GROSS_BFPD_ONDAYSAVG_WC_PCTAVG_UPTIME_DAYSMONTHS_PRODUCING_LAST_12

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
offset_producers_raw AS (
    SELECT
        cd.cmpl_nme,
        cd.cmpl_fac_id,
        cd.well_fac_id,
        cd.engr_strg_nme,
        cd.actv_indc,
        cd.in_svc_indc,
        ROUND(SQRT(
            POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
            POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
        )) AS distance_ft,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cd.cmpl_nme ORDER BY cd.actv_indc DESC, cd.cmpl_fac_id DESC) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde = 'PROD'
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 800
),
offset_producers AS (
    SELECT cmpl_nme, cmpl_fac_id, well_fac_id, engr_strg_nme, actv_indc, in_svc_indc, distance_ft
    FROM offset_producers_raw
    WHERE rn = 1
),
cum_prod AS (
    SELECT
        op.cmpl_nme,
        MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_oil_prod_vol_qty) AS cum_oil_bbl,
        MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_gros_prod_vol_qty) AS cum_gross_bbl,
        MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_wtr_prod_vol_qty) AS cum_water_bbl,
        MAX(dcm.mon_since_init_prod_qty) AS months_producing
    FROM offset_producers op
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master dcm_cm ON UPPER(op.cmpl_nme) = UPPER(dcm_cm.name)
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_mnly_fact dcm ON dcm_cm.pid = dcm.pid
    GROUP BY op.cmpl_nme
),
recent_12mo AS (
    SELECT
        op.cmpl_nme,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN mf.prod_uptm_days_qty > 0
                       THEN mf.aloc_oil_prod_vol_qty / mf.prod_uptm_days_qty
                       ELSE NULL END), 1) AS avg_oil_bopd_ondays,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN mf.prod_uptm_days_qty > 0
                       THEN mf.aloc_gros_prod_vol_qty / mf.prod_uptm_days_qty
                       ELSE NULL END), 1) AS avg_gross_bfpd_ondays,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN mf.aloc_gros_prod_vol_qty > 0
                       THEN mf.aloc_wtr_prod_vol_qty / mf.aloc_gros_prod_vol_qty * 100
                       ELSE NULL END), 1) AS avg_wc_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(mf.prod_uptm_days_qty), 1) AS avg_uptime_days,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN mf.aloc_oil_prod_vol_qty > 0 THEN 1 END) AS producing_months
    FROM offset_producers op
    JOIN dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact mf ON op.cmpl_fac_id = mf.cmpl_fac_id
    WHERE mf.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -12)
    GROUP BY op.cmpl_nme
)
SELECT
    op.cmpl_nme,
    op.cmpl_fac_id,
    op.distance_ft,
    op.actv_indc,
    op.in_svc_indc,
    wd.bore_start_dttm AS drill_date,
    ROUND(cp.cum_oil_bbl / 1000, 1) AS cum_oil_mbbl,
    ROUND(cp.cum_gross_bbl / 1000, 1) AS cum_gross_mbbl,
    cp.months_producing,
    r.avg_oil_bopd_ondays,
    r.avg_gross_bfpd_ondays,
    r.avg_wc_pct,
    r.avg_uptime_days,
    r.producing_months AS months_producing_last_12
FROM offset_producers op
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT well_fac_id, MAX(bore_start_dttm) AS bore_start_dttm
    FROM dwrptg.wlbr_dmn
    GROUP BY well_fac_id
) wd ON op.well_fac_id = wd.well_fac_id
LEFT JOIN cum_prod cp ON op.cmpl_nme = cp.cmpl_nme
LEFT JOIN recent_12mo r ON op.cmpl_nme = r.cmpl_nme
ORDER BY op.distance_ft

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
nearby_injectors_raw AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id,
           ROUND(SQRT(
               POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
               POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
           )) AS distance_ft,
           ROUND(MOD(ATAN2(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, cm.bottomx - t.bottomx) * 180 / 3.14159265358979 + 360, 360)) AS azimuth_deg,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cd.cmpl_nme ORDER BY cd.actv_indc DESC, cd.cmpl_fac_id DESC) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON UPPER(cd.cmpl_nme) = UPPER(cm.name)
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('INJ', 'STM_INJ', 'WTR_INJ')
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
),
nearby_injectors AS (
    SELECT cmpl_nme, cmpl_fac_id, distance_ft, azimuth_deg
    FROM nearby_injectors_raw
    WHERE rn = 1
)
SELECT
    ni.cmpl_nme AS injector_name,
    ni.cmpl_fac_id AS injector_fac_id,
    ni.distance_ft,
    ni.azimuth_deg,
    ROUND((500 - ni.distance_ft) / 500.0, 3) AS allocation_factor,
    MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_stm_inj_vol_qty) AS cum_steam_inj_bbl,
    MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_wtr_inj_vol_qty) AS cum_water_inj_bbl,
    ROUND(MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_stm_inj_vol_qty) * ((500 - ni.distance_ft) / 500.0), 0) AS allocated_cum_steam_bbl,
    ROUND(MAX(dcm.aloc_cum_wtr_inj_vol_qty) * ((500 - ni.distance_ft) / 500.0), 0) AS allocated_cum_water_bbl,
    MIN(dcm.eftv_dttm) AS first_inj_date,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN NVL(dcm.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 0) + NVL(dcm.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty, 0) > 0
               THEN 1 END) AS months_active
FROM nearby_injectors ni
JOIN dss.dss_completion_master dcm_cm ON UPPER(ni.cmpl_nme) = UPPER(dcm_cm.name)
JOIN dss.dss_completion_mnly_fact dcm ON dcm_cm.pid = dcm.pid
GROUP BY ni.cmpl_nme, ni.cmpl_fac_id, ni.distance_ft, ni.azimuth_deg
ORDER BY ni.distance_ft

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
nearby_injectors AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('INJ', 'STM_INJ', 'WTR_INJ')
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
    GROUP BY cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
)
SELECT
    ni.cmpl_nme AS injector_name,
    gd.lthsg_unit_nme AS zone_name,
    MAX(gd.paet_lthsg_unit_nme) AS parent_zone,
    gf.eftv_dttm,
    MAX(gf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rate_qty) AS steam_inj_rate,
    MAX(gf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rate_qty) AS water_inj_rate,
    MAX(gf.tgt_engr_stm_inj_dly_rate_qty) AS eng_target_steam,
    MAX(gf.tgt_engr_wtr_inj_dly_rate_qty) AS eng_target_water,
    CASE WHEN MAX(NVL(gf.tgt_engr_stm_inj_dly_rate_qty, 0)) > 0
         THEN ROUND(MAX(gf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rate_qty) / MAX(gf.tgt_engr_stm_inj_dly_rate_qty) * 100, 1)
         WHEN MAX(NVL(gf.tgt_engr_wtr_inj_dly_rate_qty, 0)) > 0
         THEN ROUND(MAX(gf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rate_qty) / MAX(gf.tgt_engr_wtr_inj_dly_rate_qty) * 100, 1)
         ELSE NULL END AS actual_vs_target_pct
FROM nearby_injectors ni
JOIN dwrptg.cmpl_gntl_mnly_fact gf ON ni.cmpl_fac_id = gf.cmpl_fac_id
JOIN dwrptg.gntl_dmn gd ON gd.gntl_id = gf.gntl_id
WHERE gf.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -12)
  AND NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM dwrptg.gntl_dmn child
      WHERE child.paet_gntl_id = gd.gntl_id
  )
GROUP BY ni.cmpl_nme, gd.lthsg_unit_nme, gd.paet_lthsg_unit_nme, gf.eftv_dttm
ORDER BY ni.cmpl_nme, gd.lthsg_unit_nme, gf.eftv_dttm

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
nearby_injectors AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('INJ', 'STM_INJ', 'WTR_INJ')
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
    GROUP BY cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
)
SELECT
    ni.cmpl_nme AS injector_name,
    log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm AS survey_date,
    log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt AS curve_mnemonic,
    curv.md_qty,
    curv.tvd_qty,
    MAX(curv.msd_qty) AS delta_vol_pct,
    MAX(log.surv_tbg_prsr) AS surv_tbg_prsr,

FROM nearby_injectors ni

WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt = 'ITG'
  AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
  AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL

ORDER BY ni.cmpl_nme, log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm ASC, curv.md_qty ASC

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
nearby_injectors AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('INJ', 'STM_INJ', 'WTR_INJ')
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
    GROUP BY cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
),
latest_survey AS (
    SELECT ni.cmpl_nme, ni.cmpl_fac_id,
           MAX(log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm) AS latest_date
    FROM nearby_injectors ni
    JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_dmn log ON ni.cmpl_fac_id = log.cmpl_fac_id
    WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt = 'ITG'
      AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL
    GROUP BY ni.cmpl_nme, ni.cmpl_fac_id
)
SELECT
    ls.cmpl_nme AS injector_name,
    ls.latest_date AS survey_date,
    MIN(curv.md_qty) AS survey_min_md,
    MAX(curv.md_qty) AS survey_max_md,
    ROUND(MAX(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS max_delta_vol_pct,
    MAX(curv.md_qty) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY curv.msd_qty) AS max_delta_md,
    ROUND(MIN(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS min_delta_vol_pct,
    MAX(curv.md_qty) KEEP (DENSE_RANK FIRST ORDER BY curv.msd_qty) AS min_delta_md,
    ROUND(AVG(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS avg_delta_vol_pct,
    MAX(log.surv_tbg_prsr) AS surv_tbg_prsr,
    MAX(log.surv_flw_rate_valu) AS surv_flw_rate_valu,
    COUNT(*) AS depth_stations
FROM latest_survey ls
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_dmn log ON ls.cmpl_fac_id = log.cmpl_fac_id
    AND log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm = ls.latest_date
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_msmt_fact curv ON log.log_curv_dmn_key = curv.log_curv_dmn_key
WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt = 'ITG'
  AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
  AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL
GROUP BY ls.cmpl_nme, ls.latest_date
ORDER BY ls.cmpl_nme

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
target_intervals AS (
    SELECT MIN(ow.top_md_qty) - 50 AS tgt_top_md,
           MAX(ow.btm_md_qty) + 50 AS tgt_btm_md
    FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn ow
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON ow.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND ow.actv_indc = 'Y'
      AND ow.cmpl_term_dttm IS NULL
),
nearby_obs AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('OBS', 'OBSN')
      AND SQRT(
          POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
          POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
      ) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
)
SELECT
    nob.cmpl_nme AS obs_well_name,
    log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm AS survey_date,
    log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt AS curve_mnemonic,
    ROUND(AVG(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS avg_temp_f,
    ROUND(MAX(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS max_temp_f,
    ROUND(MIN(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS min_temp_f,
    SUM(CASE WHEN curv.msd_qty > 200 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS heated_thickness_ft,
    SUM(CASE WHEN curv.msd_qty > 300 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS steam_zone_thickness_ft,
    MIN(curv.md_qty) AS survey_min_md,
    MAX(curv.md_qty) AS survey_max_md,
    COUNT(*) AS sample_count
FROM nearby_obs nob
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_dmn log ON nob.cmpl_fac_id = log.cmpl_fac_id
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_msmt_fact curv ON log.log_curv_dmn_key = curv.log_curv_dmn_key
CROSS JOIN target_intervals ti
WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt IN ('TS', 'OFOT')
  AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
  AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL
  AND curv.md_qty BETWEEN ti.tgt_top_md AND ti.tgt_btm_md
GROUP BY nob.cmpl_nme, log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm, log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt
ORDER BY nob.cmpl_nme, log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm

WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
target_intervals AS (
    SELECT MIN(ow.top_md_qty) - 50 AS tgt_top_md, MAX(ow.btm_md_qty) + 50 AS tgt_btm_md
    FROM dwrptg.actl_wlbr_opg_ntvl_dmn ow
    JOIN dwrptg.wlbr_dmn wd ON ow.wlbr_fac_id = wd.wlbr_fac_id
    WHERE wd.well_fac_id = :well_fac_id
      AND ow.cmpl_term_dttm IS NULL AND ow.actv_indc = 'Y'
),
obs_wells AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE cd.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('OBS', 'OBSN')
      AND SQRT(POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) + POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)) <= 500
      AND cd.cmpl_fac_id != :cmpl_fac_id
    GROUP BY cd.cmpl_nme, cd.cmpl_fac_id
),
latest_survey AS (
    SELECT ow.cmpl_nme, ow.cmpl_fac_id,
           MAX(log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm) AS latest_date
    FROM obs_wells ow
    JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_dmn log ON ow.cmpl_fac_id = log.cmpl_fac_id
    WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt = 'TS'
      AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
      AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL
    GROUP BY ow.cmpl_nme, ow.cmpl_fac_id
)
SELECT
    ls.cmpl_nme AS obs_well_name,
    ls.latest_date AS survey_date,
    ROUND(AVG(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS avg_temp_f,
    ROUND(MAX(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS max_temp_f,
    ROUND(MIN(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS min_temp_f,
    SUM(CASE WHEN curv.msd_qty > 200 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS heated_thickness_ft,
    SUM(CASE WHEN curv.msd_qty > 300 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS steam_zone_thickness_ft,
    MIN(curv.md_qty) AS survey_min_md,
    MAX(curv.md_qty) AS survey_max_md,
    COUNT(*) AS sample_count
FROM latest_survey ls
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_dmn log ON ls.cmpl_fac_id = log.cmpl_fac_id
    AND log.lggg_pass_strt_dttm = ls.latest_date
JOIN dwrptg.log_curv_msmt_fact curv ON log.log_curv_dmn_key = curv.log_curv_dmn_key
CROSS JOIN target_intervals ti
WHERE log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt = 'TS'
  AND log.lggg_job_id IS NOT NULL
  AND log.prcd_curv_gntn_mthd_type_desc IS NULL
  AND curv.md_qty BETWEEN ti.tgt_top_md AND ti.tgt_btm_md
GROUP BY ls.cmpl_nme, ls.latest_date
ORDER BY ls.cmpl_nme

    log.log_curv_type_mnmn_txt AS curve_mnemonic,

    ROUND(MAX(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS max_pressure_psi,
    ROUND(MIN(curv.msd_qty), 1) AS min_pressure_psi,

SQLstep_25_pattern_balance

24 months of local pattern-level aggregates — wells within 500ft in same engineering string. Total oil/gross/water production, total steam/water injection, water cut, active producer and injector counts, monthly SOR

needscmpl_nme, engr_strg_nme
row cap500
only runs whenhas_engr_strg
Producer · OR1321H-12

25 row(s), first 3 shown

EFTV_DTTMPATTERN_OIL_BOPDPATTERN_GROSS_BFPDPATTERN_WATER_CUT_PCTPATTERN_STEAM_INJ_BSPDPATTERN_WATER_INJ_BWPDPRODUCER_COUNTINJECTOR_COUNTSOR_MONTHLY
2024-08-01T00:00:00366.64642.992.11029.50612.81
2024-09-01T00:00:00420.84589.690.81024.20512.43
2024-10-01T00:00:00183.34589.4961032.90515.64
Injector · OR5580-12

25 row(s), first 3 shown

EFTV_DTTMPATTERN_OIL_BOPDPATTERN_GROSS_BFPDPATTERN_WATER_CUT_PCTPATTERN_STEAM_INJ_BSPDPATTERN_WATER_INJ_BWPDPRODUCER_COUNTINJECTOR_COUNTSOR_MONTHLY
2024-08-01T00:00:00171.24888.296.500800
2024-09-01T00:00:00153.6443496.500800
2024-10-01T00:00:00161.94510.396.400800
WITH target AS (
    SELECT bottomx, bottomy
    FROM dss.dss_completion_master
    WHERE UPPER(name) = UPPER(:cmpl_nme)
    FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
),
pattern_wells AS (
    SELECT cd.cmpl_fac_id, cd.cmpl_dmn_key, cd.prim_purp_type_cde,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cd.cmpl_nme ORDER BY cd.actv_indc DESC, cd.cmpl_fac_id DESC) AS rn
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_dmn cd
    JOIN dss.dss_completion_master cm ON cd.cmpl_nme = cm.name
    CROSS JOIN target t
    WHERE SQRT(
        POWER(cm.bottomx - t.bottomx, 2) +
        POWER(cm.bottomy - t.bottomy, 2)
    ) <= 500
      AND cd.engr_strg_nme = :engr_strg_nme
)
SELECT
    mf.eftv_dttm,
    ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty), 1) AS pattern_oil_bopd,
    ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty), 1) AS pattern_gross_bfpd,
    CASE WHEN SUM(mf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty) > 0
         THEN ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_wtr_prod_dly_rte_qty) / SUM(mf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty) * 100, 1)
         ELSE NULL END AS pattern_water_cut_pct,
    ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty), 1) AS pattern_steam_inj_bspd,
    ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty), 1) AS pattern_water_inj_bwpd,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN pw.prim_purp_type_cde = 'PROD' AND mf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
               THEN 1 END) AS producer_count,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN pw.prim_purp_type_cde IN ('INJ', 'STM_INJ', 'WTR_INJ')
                AND (NVL(mf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty, 0) + NVL(mf.aloc_wtr_inj_dly_rte_qty, 0)) > 0
               THEN 1 END) AS injector_count,
    CASE WHEN SUM(mf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty) > 0
         THEN ROUND(SUM(mf.aloc_stm_inj_dly_rte_qty) / SUM(mf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty), 2)
         ELSE NULL END AS sor_monthly
FROM pattern_wells pw
JOIN dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact mf ON pw.cmpl_dmn_key = mf.cmpl_dmn_key
WHERE pw.rn = 1
  AND mf.eftv_dttm >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -24)
GROUP BY mf.eftv_dttm
ORDER BY mf.eftv_dttm

SELECT
    pc.eftv_dttm,
    pc.bpmp_spm AS spm,
    pc.surf_bpmp_strk_lgth AS stroke_length_in,
    pc.pump_dpth AS pump_depth_ft,
    pc.dly_avg_pmp_flge AS fillage_pct,
    pc.dly_max_rod_load AS max_rod_load_lb,
    pc.dly_min_rod_load AS min_rod_load_lb,
    pc.wlhd_csg_press AS csg_press_psig,

  AND pc.eftv_dttm >= :date_range_start
ORDER BY pc.eftv_dttm DESC

The SQL that produced thisread it if you want to check exactly what is and is not included
WITH monthly_loads AS (
    SELECT
        TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM') AS month_dt,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_max_rod_load), 0) AS avg_max_load_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_min_rod_load), 0) AS avg_min_load_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_max_rod_load - pc.dly_min_rod_load), 0) AS avg_load_span_lb,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.dly_avg_pmp_flge), 1) AS avg_fillage_pct,
        ROUND(AVG(pc.bpmp_spm), 1) AS avg_spm,
        COUNT(*) AS card_count
    FROM dwrptg.dly_poc_card_anal_fact pc
    WHERE pc.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND pc.dly_max_rod_load IS NOT NULL
      AND pc.dly_min_rod_load IS NOT NULL
      AND pc.dly_max_rod_load > pc.dly_min_rod_load
    GROUP BY TRUNC(pc.eftv_dttm, 'MM')
),
monthly_prod AS (
    SELECT
        cmf.eftv_dttm AS month_dt,
        ROUND(cmf.aloc_oil_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS oil_bopd,
        ROUND(cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty, 1) AS gross_bfpd
    FROM dwrptg.cmpl_mnly_fact cmf
    WHERE cmf.cmpl_fac_id = :cmpl_fac_id
      AND cmf.aloc_gros_prod_dly_rte_qty > 0
)
SELECT
    ml.month_dt,
    ml.avg_max_load_lb,
    ml.avg_min_load_lb,
    ml.avg_load_span_lb,
    LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) AS load_span_6mo_ago,
    ml.avg_fillage_pct,
    ml.avg_spm,
    ml.card_count,
    mp.oil_bopd,
    mp.gross_bfpd,
    LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) AS gross_6mo_ago,
    CASE
        WHEN LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) IS NULL THEN 'NO_BASELINE'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
        THEN 'NARROWING'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb > LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 1.15
        THEN 'WIDENING'
        ELSE 'STABLE'
    END AS load_span_trend,
    CASE
        WHEN LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) IS NULL THEN 'NO_DATA'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb >= LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd < LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'RESERVOIR_DECLINE_NOT_PUMP'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd < LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'PUMP_DEGRADATION'
        WHEN ml.avg_load_span_lb < LAG(ml.avg_load_span_lb, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.85
             AND mp.gross_bfpd >= LAG(mp.gross_bfpd, 6) OVER (ORDER BY ml.month_dt) * 0.80
        THEN 'PUMP_WEAKENING_PROD_OK'
        ELSE 'PUMP_OK'
    END AS diagnostic_flag
FROM monthly_loads ml
LEFT JOIN monthly_prod mp ON ml.month_dt = mp.month_dt
ORDER BY ml.month_dt

step_15b_pump_param_changes
