Production Performance6 findings
  • Well shut in since 2026-06-04 (UNEC/UNECCYCL) following a multi-month DHMAINT stretch and a multi-year terminal-looking decline
  • Chronic recurring rod-pump failures (2017-2024) are the dominant driver of production instability, not a single mechanical event
  • Last well test (2026-06-04, 0 BOPD / 100% WC) is not reliable evidence the well is watered out — excluded from allocation and too short
  • Recommend fresh, valid-duration well test before any restart/revival decision
  • No cyclic steam history despite UNECCYCL shut-in code — not a CSS candidate, and steaming is regulatorily blocked regardless
  • Best proven production lever in well history: 2018 acid stim delivered a sustained +51 BOPD gain — worth re-evaluating once mechanical condition is assessed
Pump & Lift Diagnostics4 findings
  • Progressive pump-depth shallowing (1179→960 ft) coincides with terminal production collapse — likely orphaned interval below current pump setting
  • Current pump condition is unverifiable — all live diagnostic feeds are empty or frozen; last real card/production-based read is from 2024-05
  • Brief SPM reduction (7→5) reversed within 8 days in April 2023 — isolated operational trial, not a sustained trend
  • Historical pump-capacity headroom existed pre-collapse (full-card fillage in 2023) — re-evaluate only after well returns to service
Wellbore Mechanicals11 findings
  • Segmented multi-string 9.625in casing design with a full-length 7in slotted liner (624-1214ft) — the liner is the controlling restriction
  • Current pump (960ft) stands off ~251-254ft above the base of the open interval (1211-1213.6ft)
  • Pump depth chased ~200+ft shallower in 2023-2024 with two rapid-failure reinstalls before stabilizing at 960ft — cleanout/liner-integrity investigation warranted
  • Well age ~9.6yr since spud; casing/liner repair feasibility = repairable (7in liner >=5in) — no aged-casing risk
  • Abrasion-resistant Tuffer/chrome-plated pump barrels used consistently since first completion — persistent sand-service design, not a new trend
  • Completion type = full-length slotted liner (not perforated casing); no sand screens, gas anchor, or rod guides present
  • Sustained dogleg severity >3deg/100ft from 246-615ft MD (max 3.96deg/100ft) transited by unguided slick rods — rod/tubing wear risk needing failure-history correlation
  • All 4 casing strings cemented to surface with thermal (SSA-1 silica-flour) design — no isolation red flags, no CBL run
  • 519.5ft of slotted interval open across B1-E5 Tulare sands (88% of the 7in liner's span) — no closures since original 2017 completion
  • TULS_A1 (594.9-635ft, 40.1ft) — same-family Tulare sand directly updip of the open interval — never perforated/slotted; recompletion candidate pending water-contact confirmation
  • Formation tops confirm TULS_A1-E5 Tulare package to 1158ft; no OWC/BFW/fault markers recorded; shallow build-and-drop deviated profile
Engineering & Intervention Intel7 findings
  • Unretrieved fish (parted tubing + 3.75" SWOS pump) at ~1012' with possible parted liner at 1009' — fishing attempt abandoned
  • Casing/liner repair or redrill decision pending >2 years — gates all further production-optimization work
  • Recurring rod-pump/on-off-tool failures accelerated through early 2024 despite an attempted rod redesign — true root cause likely the wellbore constraint, not rod material
  • Historical acid job (2018-03-07) produced a large, sustained oil-rate increase — repeat candidate once casing/liner integrity is resolved
  • Recurring solids ingestion (fine sand, clay) managed via intake-depth/SPM changes — not an active cleanout candidate
  • Scale material recovered at 1006' co-located with the liner-top/fish zone — secondary evidence, not a standalone scale-decline candidate
  • CalGEM placeholder injection permit on file — relevant only if a future injection-conversion path is pursued
Reservoir & Spatial Context5 findings
  • Reactivate nearby injector 5177B-2 to restore lost steam support to 3153A-2
  • 3153A-2 outperformed its offset peer group before its 2023 shutdown, arguing against depletion write-off
  • Active injector 5129-2 concentrates >450% of target injection into B4_UNIT, zero to B5/C1/C2_UNIT
  • Obs well 9153-2 (66 ft) shows localized cooling 2016-2021 while 9127-2 (299 ft) shows continued heating through 2026

Temporal Context4 findings
  • Repeated rod-pump/tubing failure cluster (Feb-May 2024) drove the well to shut-in, not a single unexplained event
  • Recurring rod-pump/tubing failure cycle since 2017, unresolved by successive washbacks and pump-depth reductions
